function anzeigen(das) {
 if (document.getElementById(das).style.display=='none') {
  document.getElementById(das).style.display='block';
 }
 else {
  document.getElementById(das).style.display='none';
 }
}
</script>

<script>

    function saveSession(sport) 
    {
        $.ajax({
			url: 'sess.php',
			type: "POST",
			data: {sport_s: sport},
			success: function() {
				window.location.reload(true);
			}
		});
		
    }

</script>

<script>
$(function() {
	$('body').addClass('collapsed');
	$('.nav-toggle').on('click', function() {
		if ($('#main-navigation').hasClass('expanded')) {
			$('#main-navigation').toggleClass('expanded');
			$('body').toggleClass('expanded');
			document.cookie = "leftBar=collapsed; path=/; expires=Fri, 31 Dec 9999 23:59:59 GMT";
		} else {
			$('#main-navigation').toggleClass('expanded');
			$('body').toggleClass('expanded');
			document.cookie = "leftBar=expanded; path=/; expires=Fri, 31 Dec 9999 23:59:59 GMT";
		}
	});
	$('.nav-comp-toggle').on('click', function() {
		$('.nav-sports').hide();
		$('.nav-comps').animate({width: 'toggle'});
	});
	$('.nav-sport-toggle').on('click', function() {
		$('.nav-comps').hide();
		$('.nav-sports').animate({width: 'toggle'});
	});
	$('.nav-sport').on('click', function() {
		var sport = $(this).data('id');
		$.ajax({
			url: 'sess.php',
			type: "POST",
			data: {sport_s: sport},
			success: function() {
				window.location.reload(true);
			}
		});
	});
	
	$('.nav-search input').on('input', function() {
		var val = $(this).val().toLowerCase();
		var items = $(this).parent().parent().find('.nav-item-wrapper > *');
		
		if (val != '') {
			items.hide();
			
			items.each(function(el) {
				if ($(this).text().toLowerCase().indexOf(val) >= 0) {
					$(this).show();
				}
			});
		} else {
			items.show();
		}
	});
});

</script>

function formation(matchid) {
	$('#formations').css('display', 'flex');
	$('input[name=fmid]').val(matchid);
}
$('#close').on('click', function() {
	$('#formations').css('display', 'none');
	$('input[name=fmid]').val('');
});
$('#fm_select').on('change', function() {
	$('#formations').css('display', 'none');
	var matchid = $('input[name=fmid]').val();
	var val = $(this).val();
	//location.href = 'https://dsg-backend.com/add_formations.php?m='+matchid+'&t=1379&f=' + val;
	$(this).val('');
	window.open('https://dsg-backend.com/add_formations.php?m='+matchid+'&t=16452&f=' + val);
	
});
</script>
